CORPORATE PROFILE
企業簡介

China Lesso Group Holdings Limited (Stock Code of Hong Kong Stock                   中國聯塑集團控股有限公司（香港聯交所股份代
Exchange: 2128) is a leading large-scale industrial group that                      號：2128）是中國內地領先的大型管道建材產業集
manufactures piping and building materials in mainland China. China                 團。中國聯塑是恒生綜合中型股指數成份股之一
Lesso is one of the constituent stocks of the Hang Seng Composite                   及 MSCI 中國指數成分股之一，並獲納入為港股通
MidCap Index and MSCI China index and is a stock eligible for trading               股份。經過逾 36 年的長足發展，本集團從塑料管
through the Hong Kong Stock Exchange’s Stock Connect. After more than               道、建材家居、環保、新能源、供應鏈服務平台
36 years of rapid development, the Group has evolved into a leader in               等領域為人們提供優質的產品和服務，快速成長
the industry of building materials and home improvement. It provides                為建材家居行業的佼佼者、行業的標杆。
high-quality products and services such as plastic piping, building
materials and home improvement, new energy, environmental
protection, and operates a supply chain service platform.

Currently, the Group has established over 30 advanced production                    目 前，本 集 團 已 建 立 了 超 過 30 個 先 進 的 生 產 基
bases in 18 provinces of China and in foreign countries. The Group has              地，分佈於中國 18 個省份及海外國家。本集團擁
established a nationwide sales network and has also developed long-                 有覆蓋全國的廣泛分銷網絡，與 2,786 名的獨立獨
term strategic partnerships with 2,786 independent and exclusive first-             家一級經銷商建立長期戰略合作關係，能夠及時
tier distributors that enable timely and efficient supply of comprehensive,         及高效地為客戶提供優質豐富的產品和專業的服
quality products and professional services to customers. As an                      務。作為中國管道建材領域產品體系最為齊全的
integrated manufacturer of a comprehensive range of piping and                      生產商之一，中國聯塑提供過萬種優質產品，並
building materials, China Lesso provides over 10,000 types of quality               廣泛應用於家居裝修、民用建築、市政給水、排
products, which are widely applied to such fields as home improvement,              水、能源管理、電力通訊、燃氣、消防、環境保
civil architecture, municipal water supply, drainage, energy                        護、農業及海洋養殖等領域。
management, electric power transmission, telecommunication, gas
supply, fire services, environmental protection, agriculture and marine
aquaculture.

CHAIRMAN’S STATEMENT
主席報告書

In 2022, given that the world experienced once-in-a-century changes         2022 年，百年變局和世紀疫情交織疊加，地緣政
and pandemic as well as geopolitical uncertainty, the downward              治局勢動盪不安，世界經濟下行風險加大，國內
pressure on the global economy increased. Affected by the pandemic          經濟受到疫情和需求收縮的影響，經營發展環境
and demand contraction, the business environment in the PRC has             的複雜性、嚴峻性、不確定性上升。面對風高浪
experienced higher complexity, severity, and uncertainty. In the face of    急的國際形勢和國內經濟社會環境，2022 年國內
the challenging international situation and domestic economic and           生產總值增速下降，同比增長 3.0% 至約人民幣 121
social environment, China’s gross domestic product (GDP) growth rate        萬億元。
recorded a decline in 2022, with a year-on-year increase of 3.0% only to
approximately RMB121 trillion.

As a leading large-scale industrial conglomerate that manufactures          中國聯塑作為中國內地領先的大型管道建材產業
piping and building materials in mainland China, China Lesso capitalises    集團，把握利好的國家政策對基礎設施和管道的
on the Chinese government’s favourable policies and the stable demand       穩定需求，順勢而為，繼續發展本集團各板塊業
of the infrastructure and piping to continue developing its various         務。受到國內疫情反覆及經濟環境的影響，原材
business segments. However, the Group has faced challenges in its           料及能源價格大幅波動，加上房地產和投資氣氛
overall business performance due to the pandemic conditions and             疲軟，本集團的整體業務表現因而受到挑戰，全
economic environment in the PRC, the fluctuation of raw material and        年總收入為人民幣 307.67 億元，按年下跌 4.0%。
energy prices, as well as the sluggish real estate and investment market.   受負面市場因素拖累，本公司擁有人應佔溢利下
The Group’s total revenue for the year was RMB30,767 million,               跌 17.3% 至人民幣 25.17 億元。董事會建議宣派截
representing a year-on-year decrease of 4.0%. Profit attributable to        至 2022 年 12 月 31 日 止 年 度 之 末 期 股 息 為 每 股 30

the negative market factors. The Board proposed payment of a final

Despite numerous challenges, China Lesso forged ahead with                  雖然面對重重挑戰，但中國聯塑依然上下一心，
perseverance and overcame difficulties with a forward-looking attitude      不忘初心，砥礪前行，迎難而上，堅決為客戶和
under the collective efforts within the Group. The Group remains true to    消費者提供優質產品和服務，充分把握產業的高
its original aspirations and is committed to providing high-quality         質量發展。
products and services to customers and consumers, while fully seizing

Over the past three decades, China Lesso has continuously extended its      三十多年來，中國聯塑的產業生態鏈從塑料管道
industrial ecosystem chain from plastic piping business to building         向建材家居不斷延伸。如今本集團業務已涵蓋了

With the gradual improvement of its business layout, China Lesso has        集團開展了品牌戰略升級，對品牌定位、形象等

Potential”, etc..

Sixth Engineering Division Corp., Ltd., with plans to actively engage in     劃積極開展緊密合作和業務互動。另一方面，本
close collaboration and business interactions. On the other hand, the        集團與佛山市建設發展集團有限公司簽訂戰略合
Group entered into a strategic cooperation agreement with Foshan             作協議，雙方就建材產品、原材料採購、新能源、
Construction Development Group Co., Ltd. in respect of comprehensive         綠色建築等開展全面合作。

economy, as the pandemic prevention and control moves to a new stage           積聚增強，2023 年中國經濟將會整體好轉。
and production and living order accelerate its recovery, the endogenous
driving force of economic growth will continue to accumulate and
strengthen, and the overall Chinese economy will improve in 2023.

The management believes that China Lesso’s business fundamentals               管理層認為，多年來中國聯塑的業務基本面不斷
have been continuously optimised over the years, and the Group has             優化，具備強大的核心競爭優勢和企業韌性，對
strong core competitiveness and enterprise resilience. Therefore, the          未來長遠前景仍抱審慎樂觀的態度。本集團將堅
management remains prudently optimistic about the Group’s long-term            持繼續強化管理根基和企業基本面、提質增效、
prospects. The Group will further strengthen its management foundation         不斷提升經營質量及盈利能力，以更專業、專注、
and business fundamentals, improve quality and efficiency, and                 聚焦、精進業務，提升核心競爭力。同時，以管
continuously enhance its operational quality and profitability. The Group      道家居、智能製造、綠色能源、乘勢而為四大發
will also focus on enhancing its core competitiveness by being more            展方向進發。本集團將繼續挖掘產業機遇、推進

towards the four development directions of pipeline and home

and practices towards our shareholders, customers, business partners,          境、員工和社會的可持續發展理念和實踐工作，

SUPERVISION AND ENHANCEMENT OF RISK                                             風險管理的監督與改進
MANAGEMENT
The Group has set up basic procedures that cover the whole process of           本集團已建立貫穿於整個風險管理基本流程，連
risk management, connecting the channels of communication of risk               接各上下級、各部門和業務單位的風險管理資訊
management information along the reporting lines and among various              溝通管道，確保資訊溝通的及時、準確和完整，
departments and business units to ensure the timely, accurate and               為風險管理監督與改進奠定基礎。
complete communication of information and this has laid the foundation
for the supervision and enhancement of risk management.

The senior management of the Group, focusing on material risks,                 本集團的高級管理層以重大風險、重大事件和重
material matters and material decisions, important management matters           大決策、重要管理及業務流程為重點，對風險管
and the business flow, supervises the work relating to the first-hand           理第一手資訊收集、風險評估、風險管理策略、
information collection for risk management, risk assessment, risk               關鍵控制活動及風險管理解決方案的實施情況進
management strategies, critical control activities and the implementation       行監督。風險管理項目組每年組織對各部門和業
of risk management solutions. The risk management project team                  務單位風險管理工作實施情況和有效性進行一次
carries out an annual review and examination on the implementation of           年度檢查和檢驗，對風險管理策略進行評估，對
the works on risk management by different departments and business              跨部門和業務單位的風險管理解決方案進行評價，
units and its effectiveness for the year, assesses risk management              提出調整或改進建議，出具評價和建議報告，及
strategies and evaluates inter-departmental risk management solutions           時報送相關高級管理層。
and business units’ risk management solutions, proposes suggestions
for adjustments and improvements and issues evaluation and
recommendation reports which will be sent to the relevant senior
management in a timely manner.

MATERIAL RISKS THE GROUP MAY FACE                                               本集團可能面臨的重大風險
The material risks that the Group may face which include macro-                 本集團可能面臨的重大風險包括宏觀經濟風險、
economic risks, risks of inappropriate strategies for market competition,       市場競爭策略不當風險、原材料價格波動風險、
risks of the fluctuation of raw materials price, risks of adjustments of        環保政策、經濟政策與行業政策調整風險、網路
policies in relation to the environmental protection, economy and               安全風險、2019 冠狀病毒病（「COVID-19」）風險以
industry, cybersecurity risks, risks of the coronavirus disease (“COVID-19”),   及氣候變化風險。
and climate change risks.

The potential risks of macro-economic risks arise from the effects of           宏觀經濟風險的潛在風險產生自宏觀經濟波動的
macro-economy’s volatility and the pressure of inflation. The Group’s           影響及通貨膨脹的壓力。本集團風險應對措施包
corresponding measures on risk management include reviewing the                 括檢討宏觀經濟風險的管理策略和管理機制；檢
management strategies and mechanism for macro-economic risks;                   討收集外部宏觀經濟環境變化資訊相關機制；明
reviewing the relevant mechanism on the collection of intelligence about        確定義宏觀經濟風險分析環節；分析、檢討、調
the changes in the external macro-economic environment; clearly                 整和改善未來的宏觀經濟風險應對策略。
defining the macro-economic risk analysis session; analysing, reviewing,
adjusting and improving the strategies for managing macro-economic
risks in future.

The potential risks of the fluctuation of raw materials price arise because   原材料價格波動風險的潛在風險的產生原因為本
the price of key raw materials purchased by the Company is easily             公司採購的主要原材料價格易受提煉能力短缺、
affected by the shortfall of refining capabilities, changes in                製造商的電力及勞工成本變動、煤炭及石油化工
manufacturers’ power and labour cost structure, changes in the price of       製造的樹脂的價格變動影響。本集團風險應對措

MANAGEMENT DISCUSSION AND ANALYSIS
管理層討論及分析

MARKET OVERVIEW                                                                 市場概況
In 2022, the global economy was overshadowed by heightened                      2022 年，地緣政治緊張局勢加劇和新冠肺炎疫情
geopolitical tensions and the ongoing Covid-19 outbreak, and China              持續令全球經濟蒙上陰影，中國經濟復甦進程亦
faced multiple challenges in its economic recovery. Domestic consumer           遇到多重挑戰。由於新冠肺炎疫情在國內頻繁爆
sentiment and investment performance were very weak due to the                  發，一系列隔離措施對經濟造成沉重打擊，國內
frequent outbreaks of Covid-19 pandemic in the PRC and a series of              消費情緒及投資表現非常疲弱。受整體需求收縮、
quarantine measures that have dealt a heavy blow to the economy. The            供給承壓、預期轉弱三重壓力疊加，顯著拖累了
triple pressure of shrinking demand, supply shock and weakening                 國內經濟增長。據國家統計局公佈的資料顯示，

SHARE AWARD SCHEME                                                                    股份獎勵計劃
On 28 August 2018, the Company adopted a share award scheme (the                      於2018年8月28日，本公司採納股份獎勵計劃（「該
“Scheme”) to recognise the contributions by certain eligible persons to the           計劃」），以肯定若干合資格人士對本集團所作出

OPINION                                                                       意見
We have audited the consolidated financial statements of China Lesso          我們已審核列載於第 86 至 234 頁的中國聯塑集團

of financial position as at 31 December 2022, and the consolidated            12 月 31 日的綜合財務狀況表與截至該日止年度的

consolidated statement of changes in equity and the consolidated              綜合現金流量表，以及綜合財務報表附註，包括
statement of cash flows for the year then ended, and notes to the             主要會計政策概要。
consolidated financial statements, including a summary of significant
accounting policies.

In our opinion, the consolidated financial statements give a true and fair    我們認為，該等綜合財務報表已根據香港會計師

consolidated cash flows for the year then ended in accordance with            了 貴集團於 2022 年 12 月 31 日的綜合財務狀況及
Hong Kong Financial Reporting Standards (“HKFRSs”) issued by the Hong         截至該日止年度的綜合財務表現及綜合現金流量，
Kong Institute of Certified Public Accountants (“HKICPA”) and have been       並已遵照香港公司條例的披露規定妥為編製。

are independent of the Group in accordance with the HKICPA’s Code of           計師公會頒佈的專業會計師道德守則（「守則」），
Ethics for Professional Accountants (the “Code”), and we have fulfilled our    我們獨立於 貴集團，並已履行守則中的其他專
other ethical responsibilities in accordance with the Code. We believe         業道德責任。我們相信，我們所獲得的審核憑證
that the audit evidence we have obtained is sufficient and appropriate to      能充足及適當地為我們的審核意見提供基礎。
provide a basis for our opinion.

KEY AUDIT MATTERS                                                             關鍵審核事項
Key audit matters are those matters that, in our professional judgement,      關鍵審核事項是根據我們的專業判斷，認為對本
were of most significance in our audit of the consolidated financial          期綜合財務報表的審核最為重要的事項。這些事

 HKFRS 9 requires that the impairment of financial assets
 should be measured by the “expected credit loss” (“ECL”)
 model. In order to measure the impairment of trade and bills
 receivables and loan receivables under HKFRS 9,
 management should use judgement, assumptions and
 estimation techniques in aspects such as judging significant
 increase in credit risk, estimating the parameters for
 measuring ECLs and determining the forward-looking
 adjustments.
 香港財務報告準則第 9 號規定，金融資產的減值應以「預期
 信貸虧損」（「預期信貸虧損」）模型計量。為根據香港財務報
 告準則第 9 號計量貿易應收款項及票據和應收借款的減值，
 管理層應於多方面（如就信貸風險大幅增加作出判斷、就計
 量預期信貸虧損的參數作出估計及釐定前瞻性調整）運用判
 斷、假設及估計方法。

    Generally, there is a presumption that a majority of voting rights         於一般情況下均存在多數投票權形成控制權
    results in control. When the Company has, directly or indirectly, less     之推定。倘本公司直接或間接擁有少於投資
    than a majority of the voting or similar rights of an investee, the        對象大多數的投票或類似權利，則本集團於
    Group considers all relevant facts and circumstances in assessing          評估其是否擁有對投資對象的權力時，會考
    whether it has power over an investee, including:                          慮一切相關事實及情況，包括：

    (i)     the contractual arrangement with the other vote holders of         (i)     與投資對象其他投票持有人的合約安
            the investee;                                                              排；

    (ii)    rights arising from other contractual arrangements; and            (ii)    其他合約安排所產生的權利；及

    (iii)   the Group’s voting rights and potential voting rights.             (iii)   本集團的投票權及潛在投票權。

    The financial statements of the subsidiaries are prepared for the          附屬公司的財務報表乃就與本公司相同的報
    same reporting period as the Company, using consistent                     告期間採用一致的會計政策編製。附屬公司
    accounting policies. The results of subsidiaries are consolidated          的業績由本集團取得控制權當日起綜合入
    from the date on which the Group obtains control, and continue to          賬，並繼續綜合入賬直至該控制權終止當日
    be consolidated until the date that such control ceases.                   為止。

    Profit or loss and each component of other comprehensive income            損益及其他全面收益的各部分歸屬於本公司
    are attributed to the owners of the Company and to the non-                擁有人及非控制權益。即使導致非控制權益
    controlling interests. Total comprehensive income of subsidiaries is       出現虧絀結餘，附屬公司的全面收益總額仍
    attributable to the owners of the Company and to the non-                  歸屬於本公司擁有人及非控制權益。
    controlling interests, even if this results in the non-controlling
    interests having a deficit balance.

    When necessary, adjustments are made to the financial statements           如有需要，附屬公司的財務報表會作出調
    of subsidiaries to bring their accounting policies into line with the      整，以使其會計政策與本集團的會計政策一
    Group’s accounting policies. All intra-group assets and liabilities,       致。所有與本集團成員公司之間的交易有關

2.4 SUMMARY OF SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING                                           2.4 主要會計政策概要
    POLICIES
      INVESTMENTS IN ASSOCIATES AND JOINT VENTURES                                於聯營公司及合營企業的投資
      An associate is an entity in which the Group has a long term                聯營公司為本集團擁有一般不少於 20% 股本
      interest of generally not less than 20% of the equity voting rights         投票權的長期權益的實體，並可對其發揮重
      and over which it is in a position to exercise significant influence.       大影響力。重大影響力指參與投資對象的財
      Significant influence is the power to participate in the financial and      政及經營決策之權力，而非控制或共同控制
      operating policy decisions of the investee, but is not control or joint     該等決策之權力。
      control over those policies.

      A joint venture is a type of joint arrangement whereby the parties          合營企業為一項合營安排，據此，擁有安排
      that have joint control of the arrangement have rights to the net           之共同控制權的人士均有權享有合營企業的
      assets of the joint venture. Joint control is the contractually agreed      淨資產。共同控制乃以合約協定分佔一項安
      sharing of control of an arrangement, which exists only when                排之控制權，其僅在相關活動決策必須獲分
      decisions about the relevant activities require unanimous consent           佔控制權的人士一致同意時方存在。
      of the parties sharing control.

      The Group’s investments in associates and a joint venture are               本集團於聯營公司及一間合營企業的投資乃
      stated in the consolidated statement of financial position at the           按權益會計法核算，按本集團應佔淨資產扣
      Group’s share of net assets under the equity method of accounting,          除任何減值虧損於綜合財務狀況表中呈列。
      less any impairment losses.

      The Group’s share of the post-acquisition results and other                 本集團應佔聯營公司及合營企業收購後業績
      comprehensive income of associates and joint ventures is included           和其他全面收益分別計入損益及其他全面收
      in profit or loss and other comprehensive income, respectively. In          益內。此外，倘直接於聯營公司或合營企業
      addition, when there has been a change recognised directly in the           的權益確認變動，則本集團會於綜合權益變
      equity of the associates or joint ventures, the Group recognises its        動表確認其應佔的任何變動（如適用）。本集
      share of any changes, when applicable, in the consolidated                  團與其聯營公司或合營企業進行交易而出現
      statement of changes in equity. Unrealised gains and losses                 的未實現利益及虧損會按本集團於聯營公司
      resulting from transactions between the Group and its associates            或合營企業的權益互相抵銷，但如果未實現
      or joint ventures are eliminated to the extent of the Group’s               虧損證明所轉讓資產發生減值則除外。收購
      interests in the associates or joint ventures, except where                 聯營公司或合營企業產生的商譽計入本集團
      unrealised losses provide evidence of an impairment of the asset            於聯營公司或合營企業的投資的一部分。
      transferred. Goodwill arising from the acquisition of associates or
      joint ventures is included as part of the Group’s investments in
      associates or joint ventures.

   INTEREST IN JOINT OPERATION                                                於合營業務之權益
   A joint operation is a joint arrangement whereby the parties that          合營業務為共同安排，據此，擁有該安排之
   have joint control of the arrangement have rights to the assets, and       共同控制權的各方有權享有與該安排有關的
   obligations for the liabilities, relating to the arrangement. Joint        資產及就負債承擔責任。共同控制乃以合約
   control is the contractually agreed sharing of control of an               協定分佔一項安排之控制權，其僅在相關活
   arrangement, which exists only when decisions about the relevant           動決策必須獲分佔控制權的人士一致同意時
   activities require the unanimous consent of the parties sharing            方存在。
   control.

   The Group recognises in relation to its interest in a joint operation:     本集團就其於合營業務之權益確認：

   •     its assets, including its share of any assets held jointly;          •   其資產，包括分佔任何共同持有之資
                                                                                  產；

   •     its liabilities, including its share of any liabilities incurred     •   其負債，包括分佔任何共同產生之負
         jointly;                                                                 債；

   •     its revenue from the sale of its share of the output arising         •   出售其於合營業務應佔產出之所得收
         from the joint operation;                                                入；

   •     its share of the revenue from the sale of the output by the          •   出售合營業務產出之應佔收入；及
         joint operation; and

   •     its expenses, including its share of any expenses incurred           •   其開支，包括分佔任何共同產生之開
         jointly.                                                                 支。

   The assets, liabilities, revenues and expenses relating to the             本集團於合營業務之權益之有關資產、負
   Group’s interest in a joint operation are accounted for in                 債、收入及開支乃根據適用於相關資產、負
   accordance with the HKFRSs applicable to the particular assets,            債、收入及開支之香港財務報告準則入賬。
   liabilities, revenues and expenses.

2.4 SUMMARY OF SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING                                      2.4 主要會計政策概要（續）
    POLICIES (Continued)
   BUSINESS COMBINATIONS AND GOODWILL                                        業務合併及商譽
   Business combinations are accounted for using the acquisition             業務合併乃以購買法入賬。轉讓之代價乃以
   method. The consideration transferred is measured at the                  收購日期的公允價值計算，該公允價值為本
   acquisition-date fair value which is the sum of the acquisition-date      集團轉讓的資產於收購日期的公允價值、本
   fair values of assets transferred by the Group, liabilities assumed       集團自被收購方之前度擁有人承擔的負債，
   by the Group to the former owners of the acquiree and the equity          及本集團發行以換取被收購方控制權的股本
   interests issued by the Group in exchange for control of the              權益的總和。於各業務合併中，本集團選擇
   acquiree. For each business combination, the Group elects                 是否以公允價值或被收購方可識別資產淨值
   whether to measure the non-controlling interests in the acquiree          的應佔比例，計算屬現時擁有權權益並賦予
   that are present ownership interests and entitle their holders to a       其持有人有權於清盤時按比例分佔實體的淨
   proportionate share of net assets in the event of liquidation either      資產之被收購方的非控制權益。非控制權益
   at fair value or at the proportionate share of the acquiree’s             的一切其他成分乃按公允價值計量。收購相
   identifiable net assets. All other components of non-controlling          關成本於產生時列為開支。
   interests are measured at fair value. Acquisition-related costs are
   expensed as incurred.

   The Group determines that it has acquired a business when the             當所取得的一組活動及資產包括一項投入和
   acquired set of activities and assets includes an input and a             一項重要過程，而兩者共同對形成產出的能
   substantive process that together significantly contribute to the         力有重大貢獻，則本集團釐定為業務收購。
   ability to create outputs.

   When the Group acquires a business, it assesses the financial             當本集團收購一項業務時，會根據合約條
   assets and liabilities assumed for appropriate classification and         款、於收購日期的經濟環境及相關條件，評
   designation in accordance with the contractual terms, economic            估將承接的金融資產及負債，以作出適當的
   circumstances and pertinent conditions as at the acquisition date.        分類及標示，其中包括分離被收購方主合約
   This includes the separation of embedded derivatives in host              中的嵌入式衍生工具。
   contracts of the acquiree.

   If the business combination is achieved in stages, the previously         倘業務合併分階段進行，以往持有的股本權
   held equity interest is remeasured at its acquisition-date fair value     益會以收購日期的公允價值重新計量，任何
   and any resulting gain or loss is recognised in profit or loss.           因此帶來的利益或虧損於損益中確認。

   Any contingent consideration to be transferred by the acquirer is         將由收購方轉讓的任何或然代價於收購日期
   recognised at fair value at the acquisition date. Contingent              按公允價值確認。或然代價（分類為資產或
   consideration classified as an asset or liability is measured at fair     負債）按公允價值計量，而公允價值變動則
   value with changes in fair value recognised in profit or loss.            於損益確認。分類為權益之或然代價並不會
   Contingent consideration that is classified as equity is not              重新計量，其後結算於權益中入賬。
   remeasured and subsequent settlement is accounted for within
   equity.

2.4 SUMMARY OF SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING                                       2.4 主要會計政策概要（續）
    POLICIES (Continued)
   BUSINESS COMBINATIONS AND GOODWILL (Continued)                             業務合併及商譽（續）
   Goodwill is initially measured at cost, being the excess of the            商譽初始按成本計量，即已轉讓代價、已確
   aggregate of the consideration transferred, the amount recognised          認非控制權益金額及本集團先前持有被收購
   for non-controlling interests and any fair value of the Group’s            方股本權益的任何公允價值之總額超出所收
   previously held equity interests in the acquiree over the identifiable     購可識別資產及所承擔負債的差額。倘此代
   assets acquired and liabilities assumed. If the sum of this                價及其他項目的總和低於所收購淨資產的公
   consideration and other items is lower than the fair value of the net      允價值，則再評估後的差額於損益確認為議
   assets acquired, the difference is, after reassessment, recognised         價收購所得利益。
   in profit or loss as a gain on bargain purchase.

   After initial recognition, goodwill is measured at cost less any           於初始確認後，商譽按成本減任何累計減值
   accumulated impairment losses. Goodwill is tested for impairment           虧損計量。商譽須每年進行減值測試，若有
   annually or more frequently if events or changes in circumstances          事件發生或情況改變顯示賬面值可能減值，
   indicate that the carrying value may be impaired. The Group will           則會更頻密地進行測試。本集團將於 12 月 31
   perform its annual impairment test of goodwill as at 31 December.          日進行商譽的年度減值測試。為進行減值測
   For the purpose of impairment testing, goodwill acquired in a              試，無論本集團其他資產或負債是否已分配
   business combination is, from the acquisition date, allocated to           至現金產生單位或現金產生單位組別，因業
   each of the Group’s cash-generating units, or groups of cash-              務合併而購入的商譽自收購日期起分配至預
   generating units, that are expected to benefit from the synergies of       期可從合併產生的協同效益中獲益的本集團
   the combination, irrespective of whether other assets or liabilities       各現金產生單位或現金產生單位組別。
   of the Group are assigned to those units or groups of units.

   Impairment is determined by assessing the recoverable amount of           減值乃通過評估與商譽有關的現金產生單位
   the cash-generating unit (group of cash-generating units) to which        （現金產生單位組別）的可收回金額釐定。當
   the goodwill relates. Where the recoverable amount of the cash-           現金產生單位（現金產生單位組別）的可收回
   generating unit (group of cash-generating units) is less than the         金額低於賬面值時，則確認減值虧損。已確
   carrying amount, an impairment loss is recognised. An impairment          認商譽減值虧損不得於其後期間撥回。
   loss recognised for goodwill is not reversed in a subsequent
   period.

   Where goodwill has been allocated to a cash-generating unit (or            倘商譽已分配至現金產生單位（或現金產生
   group of cash-generating units) and part of the operation within           單位組別）而該單位的部分業務已出售，則
   that unit is disposed of, the goodwill associated with the operation       與所出售業務相關的商譽會在釐定出售的利
   disposed of is included in the carrying amount of the operation            益或虧損時計入該業務的賬面值。在該等情
   when determining the gain or loss on the disposal. Goodwill                況下出售的商譽，乃根據所出售業務的相對
   disposed of in these circumstances is measured based on the                價值及現金產生單位的保留份額計量。
   relative value of the operation disposed of and the portion of the
   cash-generating unit retained.

   FAIR VALUE MEASUREMENT                                                       公允價值之計量
   The Group measures its investment properties, asset held for sale,           本集團於各報告期末按公允價值計量其投資
   other financial assets and other financial liabilities at fair value at      物業、持作出售資產、其他金融資產及其他
   the end of each reporting period. Fair value is the price that would         金融負債。公允價值為市場參與者於計量日
   be received to sell an asset or paid to transfer a liability in an           期在有序交易中出售資產所收取的價格或轉
   orderly transaction between market participants at the                       讓負債所支付的價格。公允價值計量乃假設
   measurement date. The fair value measurement is based on the                 出售資產或轉讓負債的交易於資產或負債主
   presumption that the transaction to sell the asset or transfer the           要市場或（在無主要市場情況下）最具優勢市
   liability takes place either in the principal market for the asset or        場進行而作出。主要或最具優勢市場須為本
   liability, or in the absence of a principal market, in the most              集團可進入之市場。資產或負債的公允價值
   advantageous market for the asset or liability. The principal or the         乃基於市場參與者於資產或負債定價所用的
   most advantageous market must be accessible by the Group. The                假設計量（即假設市場參與者會以最佳經濟
   fair value of an asset or a liability is measured using the                  利益行事）。
   assumptions that market participants would use when pricing the
   asset or liability, assuming that market participants act in their
   economic best interest.

   A fair value measurement of a non-financial asset takes into                 非金融資產的公允價值計量須計及市場參與
   account a market participant’s ability to generate economic                  者透過最大限度使用該資產達致最佳用途，
   benefits by using the asset in its highest and best use or by selling        或將該資產出售予將最大限度使用該資產達
   it to another market participant that would use the asset in its             致最佳用途的其他市場參與者以產生經濟利
   highest and best use.                                                        益的能力。

   The Group uses valuation techniques that are appropriate in the              本集團採納適用於不同情況且具備充分數據
   circumstances and for which sufficient data are available to                 以供計量公允價值的估值方法，以盡量使用
   measure fair value, maximising the use of relevant observable                相關可觀察輸入數據及盡量減少使用不可觀
   inputs and minimising the use of unobservable inputs.                        察輸入數據。

   All assets and liabilities for which fair value is measured or               所有於本綜合財務報表計量或披露公允價值
   disclosed in the consolidated financial statements are categorised           的資產及負債乃基於對公允價值計量整體而
   within the fair value hierarchy, described as follows, based on the          言屬重大的最低層輸入數據按以下公允價值
   lowest level input that is significant to the fair value measurement         架構分類：
   as a whole:

   Level 1   —     based on quoted prices (unadjusted) in active                第一級 — 基於相同資產或負債於活躍市
                   markets for identical assets or liabilities                        場的報價（未經調整）

   Level 2   —     based on valuation techniques for which the                  第二級 — 按估值方法計量，而對公允價
                   lowest level input that is significant to the fair value           值計量而言屬重大的最低層輸
                   measurement is observable, either directly or                      入數據可直接或間接觀察得出
                   indirectly

   Level 3   —     based on valuation techniques for which the                  第三級 — 按估值方法計量，而對公允價
                   lowest level input that is significant to the fair value           值計量而言屬重大的最低層輸
                   measurement is unobservable                                        入數據不可觀察得出

   asset’s recoverable amount is estimated. An asset’s recoverable           出估計。資產的可收回金額為資產或現金產

   the asset. An impairment loss is charged to profit or loss in the          間於損益內與減值資產的功能一致的開支類

   with the function of the impaired asset.

    POLICIES (Continued)
   INVESTMENTS AND OTHER FINANCIAL ASSETS                                       投資及其他金融資產
   Initial recognition and measurement                                          初始確認及計量
   Financial assets are classified, at initial recognition, as                  金融資產於初始確認時分類為其後按攤銷成
   subsequently measured at amortised cost, fair value through other            本計量、按公允價值計入其他全面收益（「按
   comprehensive income (“FVTOCI”), and fair value through profit or            公允價值計入其他全面收益」）及按公允價值
   loss (“FVTPL”).                                                              計入損益（「按公允價值計入損益」）。

   The classification of financial assets at initial recognition depends        於初始確認時，金融資產分類視乎金融資產
   on the financial asset’s contractual cash flow characteristics and           的合約現金流量特徵及本集團管理該等金融
   the Group’s business model for managing them. With the                       資產的業務模式而定。除並無包含重大融資
   exception of trade and bills receivables that do not contain a               成分的貿易應收款項及票據或本集團已就貿
   significant financing component or for which the Group has                   易應收款項及票據應用不調整重大融資成分
   applied the practical expedient of not adjusting the effect of a             影響的可行權宜方法外，本集團初始按公允
   significant financing component, the Group initially measures a              價值（倘為並非按公允價值計入損益的金融
   financial asset at its fair value plus, in the case of a financial asset     資產則另加交易成本）計量金融資產。並無
   not at FVTPL, transaction costs. Trade and bills receivables that do         包含重大融資成分或本集團已應用可行權宜
   not contain a significant financing component or for which the               方法的貿易應收款項及票據根據香港財務報
   Group has applied the practical expedient are measured at the                告準則第 15 號（按照下文「收入確認」所載的
   transaction price determined under HKFRS 15 in accordance with               政策）釐定的交易價格計量。
   the policies set out for “Revenue recognition” below.

   In order for a financial asset to be classified and measured at              為將金融資產按攤銷成本或按公允價值計入
   amortised cost or FVTOCI, it needs to give rise to cash flows that           其他全面收益進行分類及計量，金融資產需
   are solely payments of principal and interest (“SPPI”) on the                產生純粹為支付本金及未償還本金之利息
   principal amount outstanding. Financial assets with cash flows that         （「純粹支付本金及利息」）的現金流量。現金
   are not SPPI are classified and measured at FVTPL, irrespective of           流量並非純粹支付本金及利息之金融資產按
   the business model.                                                          公允價值計入損益進行分類及計量，而不論
                                                                                業務模式。

   The Group’s business model for managing financial assets refers              本集團管理金融資產的業務模式指其如何管
   to how it manages its financial assets in order to generate cash             理金融資產以產生現金流量。業務模式釐定
   flows. The business model determines whether cash flows will                 現金流量會否產生自收取合約現金流量、出
   result from collecting contractual cash flows, selling the financial         售金融資產，或兩者兼有。按攤銷成本進行
   assets, or both. Financial assets classified and measured at                 分類及計量的金融資產按持有金融資產以收
   amortised cost are held within a business model with the objective           取合約現金流量的業務模式持有，而按公允
   to hold financial assets in order to collect contractual cash flows,         價值計入其他全面收益進行分類及計量的金
   while financial assets classified and measured at FVTOCI are held            融資產按旨在持有金融資產以收取合約現金
   within a business model with the objective of both holding to                流量及出售金融資產的業務模式持有。並非
   collect contractual cash flows and selling. Financial assets which           按上述業務模式持有的金融資產為按公允價
   are not held within the aforementioned business models are                   值計入損益進行分類及計量。
   classified and measured at FVTPL.

   All regular way purchases and sales of financial assets are             所有以日常買賣之金融資產，均按交易日（即

   Subsequent measurement                                                  其後計量

2.4 SUMMARY OF SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING                                        2.4 主要會計政策概要（續）
    POLICIES (Continued)
   IMPAIRMENT OF FINANCIAL ASSETS                                              金融資產減值
   The Group recognises an allowance for expected credit losses                本集團就並非按公允價值計入損益持有的所
   (“ECLs”) for all debt instruments not held at FVTPL. ECLs are based         有債務工具確認預期信貸虧損（「預期信貸虧
   on the difference between the contractual cash flows due in                 損」）撥備。預期信貸虧損乃基於根據合約應
   accordance with the contract and all the cash flows that the Group          付的合約現金流量與本集團預期收取的所有
   expects to receive, discounted at an approximation of the original          現金流量之間的差額，並按接近原有實際利
   effective interest rate. The expected cash flows will include cash          率貼現。預期現金流量將包括出售所持抵押
   flows from the sale of collateral held or other credit enhancements         品或合約條款所包含的其他信貸增強措施所
   that are integral to the contractual terms.                                 得的現金流量。

   General approach                                                            一般方法
   ECLs are recognised in two stages. For credit exposures for which           預期信貸虧損分兩個階段確認。就自初始確
   there has not been a significant increase in credit risk since initial      認以來信貸風險並無大幅增加的信貸風險而
   recognition, ECLs are provided for credit losses that result from           言，會為未來 12 個月內的可能違約事件所產
   default events that are possible within the next 12 months (a               生的信貸虧損計提預期信貸虧損（12 個月預
   12-month ECL). For those credit exposures for which there has been          期信貸虧損）。就自初始確認以來信貸風險
   a significant increase in credit risk since initial recognition, a loss     大幅增加的信貸風險而言，不論違約時間如
   allowance is required for credit losses expected over the remaining         何，須就預期於風險餘下存續期內的信貸虧
   life of the exposure, irrespective of the timing of the default (a          損計提虧損撥備（整個存續期預期信貸虧損） 。
   lifetime ECL).

   At each reporting date, the Group assesses whether the credit risk          於各報告日期，本集團評估自初始確認後金
   on a financial instrument has increased significantly since initial         融工具的信貸風險是否大幅增加。於進行評
   recognition. When making the assessment, the Group compares                 估時，本集團將於報告日期金融工具發生違
   the risk of a default occurring on the financial instrument as at the       約的風險與初始確認日期金融工具發生違約
   reporting date with the risk of a default occurring on the financial        的風險進行比較，並考慮所有毋須付出不必
   instrument as at the date of initial recognition and considers              要成本或努力即可獲得的合理支持資料，包
   reasonable and supportable information that is available without            括歷史及前瞻性資料。當合約付款已逾期超
   undue cost or effort, including historical and forward-looking              過 30 天，本集團認為信貸風險已大幅增加。
   information. The Group considers that there has been a significant
   increase in credit risk when contractual payments are more than
   30 days past due.

   The Group considers a financial asset in default when contractual           當合約付款已逾期 90 天，本集團認為金融
   payments are 90 days past due. However, in certain cases, the               資產將會違約。可是，於某些情況下，倘內
   Group may also consider a financial asset to be in default when             部或外部資料顯示在並無計及本集團持有的
   internal or external information indicates that the Group is unlikely       任何信貸增強措施前，本集團不大可能悉數
   to receive the outstanding contractual amounts in full before taking        收到未償還合約款項，本集團亦可能認為金
   into account any credit enhancements held by the Group.                     融資產將會違約。

   The subsequent measurement of financial liabilities depends on              金融負債的其後計量視乎下述分類而定：
   their classification, as described below:

   Financial liabilities at fair value through profit or loss                  按公允價值計入損益的金融負債
   Financial liabilities at FVTPL include financial liabilities held for       按公允價值計入損益的金融負債包括持作買
   trading and financial liabilities designated upon initial recognition       賣的金融負債及於初始確認時指定為按公允
   as at FVTPL.                                                                價值計入損益的金融負債。

   Financial liabilities are classified as held for trading if they are        倘為於近期購回而產生金融負債，則金融負
   incurred for the purpose of repurchasing in the near term. This             債分類為持作買賣。此類別亦包括本集團所
   category also includes derivative financial instruments entered into        訂立於香港財務報告準則第 9 號所界定的對
   by the Group that are not designated as hedging instruments in              沖關係中並無指定為對沖工具的衍生金融工
   hedge relationships as defined by HKFRS 9. Separated embedded               具。獨立嵌入式衍生工具亦分類為持作買
   derivatives are also classified as held for trading unless they are         賣，惟指定為有效對沖工具的衍生工具則除
   designated as effective hedging instruments.                                外。

   Gains or losses on liabilities held for trading are recognised in           持作買賣的負債利益或虧損確認於損益。已
   profit or loss. The net fair value gain or loss recognised in profit or     於損益確認的公允價值淨利益或淨虧損並不
   loss does not include any interest charged on these financial               包括任何該金融負債的利息。
   liabilities.

   Financial liabilities designated upon initial recognition as at FVTPL       於初始確認時指定為按公允價值計入損益的
   are designated at the date of initial recognition and only if the           金融負債於初始確認日期指定，並僅於符合
   criteria in HKFRS 9 are satisfied.                                          香港財務報告準則第 9 號標準的情況下方會
                                                                               進行。

   Financial liabilities at amortised cost (loans and borrowings)              按攤銷成本列賬的金融負債（貸款及借款）
   After initial recognition, interest-bearing loans and borrowings are        於初始確認後，計息貸款及借款其後以實際
   subsequently measured at amortised cost using the effective                 利率（「實際利率」）法按攤銷成本計量。終止
   interest rate (“EIR”) method. Gains and losses are recognised in            確認負債及於實際利率攤銷過程中產生的利
   profit or loss when the liabilities are derecognised as well as             益及虧損於損益確認。
   through the EIR amortisation process.

   Amortised cost is calculated by taking into account any discount or         計算攤銷成本時會考慮收購所產生的任何折
   premium on acquisition and fees or costs that are an integral part          讓或溢價及構成實際利率組成部分的費用或
   of the EIR. The EIR amortisation is included as finance costs in profit     成本。實際利率攤銷計入於損益內的融資成
   or loss.                                                                    本。

   CASH AND CASH EQUIVALENTS                                                現金及現金等價物
   Cash and cash equivalents comprise cash on hand and demand               現金及現金等價物包括現金及活期存款，以

   convertible into known amounts of cash, are subject to an                險極低及一般自購入後三個月內到期的短期

   PROVISIONS                                                               撥備
   A provision is recognised when a present obligation (legal or            倘因過往事件導致現有債務（法定或推定）及
   constructive) has arisen as a result of a past event and it is           日後可能需要有資源流出以償還債務，則確
   probable that a future outflow of resources will be required to          認撥備，但必須能可靠估計有關債務金額。
   settle the obligation, provided that a reliable estimate can be made
   of the amount of the obligation.

   When the effect of discounting is material, the amount recognised        倘折現的影響重大，則就撥備確認的金額為
   for a provision is the present value at the end of the reporting         預期需用作償還債務的未來支出於報告期末
   period of the future expenditures expected to be required to settle      的現值。因時間流逝而產生的折現現值增額
   the obligation. The increase in the discounted present value             會計入損益內的融資成本。
   amount arising from the passage of time is included in finance
   costs in profit or loss.

   of the level of repairs and returns, discounted to their present

   customer. The variable consideration is estimated at contract             並受其約束，直至其後與可變代價相關的不
   inception and constrained until it is highly probable that a              確定性消除時確認的累計收入金額極有可能
   significant revenue reversal in the amount of cumulative revenue          不會發生收入大幅撥回。
   recognised will not occur when the associated uncertainty with the
   variable consideration is subsequently resolved.

   When the contract contains a financing component which provides           當合約包含融資部分，就向客戶轉讓貨品或
   the customer with a significant benefit of financing the transfer of      服務而為客戶提供重大融資利益超過一年
   goods or services to the customer for more than one year, revenue         時，收入按應收金額的現值計量，並按合約
   is measured at the present value of the amount receivable,                生效時在本集團與客戶之間訂立的獨立融資
   discounted using the discount rate that would be reflected in a           交易中反映的貼現率貼現。當合約包含為本
   separate financing transaction between the Group and the                  集團提供超過一年重大融資利益的融資部分
   customer at contract inception. When the contract contains a              時，根據合約確認的收入包括根據實際利率
   financing component which provides the Group with a significant           法計算的合約負債所附加的利息開支。對於
   financial benefit for more than one year, revenue recognised under        客戶付款與承諾轉讓貨品或服務之間的期限
   the contract includes the interest expense accreted on the contract       為一年或不足一年的合約，交易價格不會採
   liability under the effective interest method. For a contract where       用香港財務報告準則第 15 號的可行權宜方法
   the period between the payment by the customer and the transfer           就重大融資部分的影響作出調整。
   of the promised goods or services is one year or less, the
   transaction price is not adjusted for the effects of a significant
   financing component, using the practical expedient in HKFRS 15.

   the Group’s best estimate of the number of equity instruments that            入賬的金額指期初及期末已確認累計開支的
   will ultimately vest. The charge or credit to profit or loss for a period     變動。
   represents the movement in the cumulative expense recognised as

   account when determining the grant date fair value of awards, but             慮服務及非市場表現條件，但會評估達成該

   FOREIGN CURRENCIES                                                         外幣

     Leases — Estimating the incremental borrowing rate                            租賃 — 估算增量借款利率
     The Group cannot readily determine the interest rate implicit in a            本集團無法輕易釐定租賃內所隱含的利率，
     lease, and therefore, it uses an incremental borrowing rate (“IBR”)           因此使用增量借款利率（「增量借款利率」）計
     to measure lease liabilities. The IBR is the rate of interest that the        量租賃負債。增量借款利率為本集團於類似
     Group would have to pay to borrow over a similar term, and with a             經濟環境中為取得與使用權資產價值相近之
     similar security, the funds necessary to obtain an asset of a similar         資產，而以類似借期與類似抵押品借入所需
     value to the right-of-use asset in a similar economic environment.            資金應支付之利率。因此，增量借款利率反
     The IBR therefore reflects what the Group “would have to pay”,                映了本集團「應支付」的款項，當無可觀察
     which requires estimation when no observable rates are available              的利率時（如就並無訂立融資交易之附屬公
     (such as for subsidiaries that do not enter into financing                    司而言）或當須對利率進行調整以反映租賃
     transactions) or when it needs to be adjusted to reflect the terms            之條款及條件時（如當租賃並非以附屬公司
     and conditions of the lease (for example, when leases are not in              之功能貨幣訂立時），則須對利率作出估計。
     the subsidiary’s functional currency). The Group estimates the IBR            當可觀察輸入數據（如市場利率）可用時，本
     using observable inputs (such as market interest rates) when                  集團用此估算增量借款利率，並須作出若干
     available and is required to make certain entity-specific estimates           實體特定的估計（如附屬公司的獨立信貸評
     (such as the subsidiary’s stand-alone credit rating).                         級）。

     Performance obligations                                                       履約責任
     Information about the Group’s performance obligations is                      有關本集團履約責任的資料概述如下：
     summarised below:

     Sale of goods                                                                 銷售貨品
     The performance obligation is satisfied upon delivery of the goods            本集團於交貨時履行責任，付款一般在交貨
     and payment is generally due within 30 to 360 days from delivery              後 30 至 360 天內到期，或視乎市況、市埸策
     or is settled on an advance receipt basis depending on the market             略及與客戶的關係按預收方式結算。
     conditions, market tactics and relationships with the customers.

     Renovation, installation and engineering services                             裝修、安裝及工程服務
     The performance obligation is satisfied over time as services are             履約責任隨提供服務而隨時間推移履行，且
     rendered and payment is generally due within 30 days from the                 付款一般於結算日起 30 天內到期。客戶保
     date of billing. A certain percentage of payment is retained by               留一定百分比的付款直至保留期結束，乃由
     customers until the end of the retention period as the Group’s                於本集團獲取最終付款的權利視乎於客戶按
     entitlement to the final payment is conditional on the satisfaction of        合約規定的一段時間內對服務質素表示滿意
     the service quality by the customers over a certain period as                 後方可作實。
     stipulated in the contracts.

     Logistics and other related services                                          物流及其他相關服務
     The performance obligation is satisfied over time as services are             履約責任隨著提供服務的時間推移履行，且
     rendered and payment is generally due within 30 days upon                     付款一般於服務完成後 30 天內到期或視乎
     completion of services or is settled on an advance receipt basis              與客戶的關係按預收方式結算。
     depending on the relationships with the customers.

     The amounts of transaction prices allocated to the remaining                  於報告期末，分配至餘下履約責任（未履行

34. DEFERRED TAX (Continued)                                                  34. 遞延稅項（續）
    Pursuant to the PRC Corporate Income Tax Law, a 10% withholding             根據中國企業所得稅法，在中國內地成立的
    tax is levied on dividends declared to foreign investors from the           外資企業向外國投資者宣派的股息須繳納
    foreign investment enterprises established in mainland China. The           10% 的預提所得稅。該規定自 2008 年 1 月 1 日
    requirement is effective from 1 January 2008 and applies to                 起 生 效，適 用 於 自 2007 年 12 月 31 日 起 獲 得
    earnings after 31 December 2007. A lower withholding tax rate               的盈利。倘中國內地與該外國投資者所在司
    may be applied if there is a tax treaty between mainland China              法權區有簽訂稅務條約，則可按較低稅率繳
    and the jurisdictions of the foreign investors. The applicable rate         納預提所得稅。本集團的適用稅率為 5%。
    for the Group is 5%. The Group is therefore liable for withholding          因此，就該等於中國內地成立的附屬公司就
    taxes on dividends distributed by those subsidiaries established in         2008 年 1 月 1 日起所得盈利而分派的股息而
    mainland China in respect of their earnings generated from 1                言，本集團須繳納預提所得稅。
    January 2008.

    At the end of the reporting period, the Group has not recognised             於報告期末，由於本公司控制該等附屬公司
    deferred tax liabilities of RMB279,496,000 (2021: RMB343,290,000)            的股息政策，且該等溢利於可見將來分派
    in respect of temporary differences relating to the undistributed            的 可 能 性 不 大， 故 本 集 團 並 無 確 認 該 等
    profits of subsidiaries, amounting to RMB5,589,928,000 (2021:                附屬公司未分派溢利暫時差異為人民幣
    RMB6,865,804,000), that would be payable on the distribution of              5,589,928,000 元（2021 年： 人 民 幣
    these retained profits as the Company controls the dividend policy           6,865,804,000 元）的遞延稅項負債（須於分派
    of these subsidiaries and it is probable that these profits will not be      該等保留溢利時支付）人民幣 279,496,000 元
    distributed in the foreseeable future.                                      （2021 年：人民幣 343,290,000 元）。

35. DEFERRED INCOME                                                           35. 遞延收益
    Deferred income represents government grants received by the                遞延收益指本集團收取的政府補助，作為其
    Group as financial subsidies for the renovation of factories and            在廣東、長春、湖南、海南、大慶、湖北及
    machineries or the construction of new factory premises in                  雲南等翻新工廠及機械或興建新工廠物業的
    Guangdong, Changchun, Hunan, Hainan, Daqing, Hubei, Yunnan,                 財政補貼，以及自本集團向若干僱員預先收
    etc. and rental income received in advance from certain of the              取的租賃員工宿舍租金收益。
    Group’s employees for leasing the staff quarters.

    The government grants are released to profit or loss over the               政府補助於有關房產預期可使用年限內按年
    expected useful lives of the relevant premises by equal annual              以等額撥入損益內確認，而預先收取的租金
    instalments, while rental income received in advance is recognised          收益則按各自的租期以直線法確認。
    on the straight-line basis over the respective lease terms.

   The Group’s principal financial instruments comprise borrowings                本集團的主要金融工具包括借款以及現金及
   and cash and short-term deposits. The main purpose of these                    短期存款。該等金融工具的主要用途乃為本

   minimum, the Group has not used any derivatives and other                      險維持於最低水平，本集團並無使用任何衍

   CREDIT RISK                                                                   信貸風險
   Credit risk is the risk that a counterparty will not meet its obligations     信貸風險指因交易對手無法履行其於金融工
   under a financial instrument or customer contract, leading to a               具或客戶合約項下責任而蒙受財務虧損的風
   financial loss. The Group is exposed to credit risk in relation to its        險。本集團所面臨的信貸風險與其貿易應收
   trade and bills receivables, contract assets, loan receivables, other         款項及票據、合約資產、應收借款、其他應
   receivables, debt instruments at FVTOCI and cash and bank                     收款項、按公允價值計入其他全面收益的債
   deposits.                                                                     務工具以及現金及銀行存款有關。

   The concession operating right of the Group represents sewage                 本集團的特許經營權指有關建造、營運及移
   treatment service concession financial receivables in respect of              交（「建造、營運及移交」）安排的特許污水處
   Build-operate-transfer (“BOT”) arrangements. Since the parties to             理服務的金融應收款項。於報告期末，鑑於
   BOT arrangements are local government authorities in the PRC and              建造、營運及移交安排的訂約方均為中國及
   Thailand, the Group considers the credit risk is low at the end of            泰國地方政府機關，故本集團認為信貸風險
   the reporting period. The Group does not hold any collateral over             偏低。本集團並無就有關結餘持有任何抵押
   these balances.                                                               品。

   In relation to the Group’s financial service business, the Group has          有關本集團的金融服務業務，本集團擁有信
   a credit team which is responsible for the evaluation of customers’           貸團隊負責評估客戶的信貸評級、財務背景
   credit ratings, financial background and repayment abilities. The             及償還能力。本集團僅向信譽良好的客戶提
   Group only provides credit to customers with creditworthiness and             供信貸及一般須提供抵押品。除具特定信貸
   collateral is normally required. Except for the individual customers          風險且其結餘已減值的個別客戶外，該等客
   with specific credit risk and whose balances have been impaired,              戶的付款拖欠風險被視為並不重大。
   the risk of default in payment by these customers is considered not
   significant.

   The Group has certain concentration of credit risk as the Group’s             由於本集團的主要客戶來自房地產或與其相
   major customers are from or are related to the real estate industry,          關的行業，本集團有若干信貸風險集中情
   but the Group has policies in place to ensure that sales are made             況，惟本集團訂有政策以確保向擁有穩健財
   to purchasers with an appropriate financial strength and                      政實力及可支付適當百分比之首期付款的買
   appropriate percentage of down payments. It also has other                    方作出銷售，亦設有其他監控程序以確保採
   monitoring procedures to ensure that follow-up action is taken to             取跟進行動收回逾期債務。此外，本集團定
   recover overdue debts. In addition, the Group reviews regularly the           期審閱各項個別貿易應收款項及票據和合約
   recoverable amount of each individual trade and bills receivables             資產的可收回金額，以確保就不可收回金額
   and contract assets to ensure that adequate impairment losses                 作出足夠的減值虧損。本集團的信貸風險源
   are made for irrecoverable amounts. The Group has no significant              自多名交易對手及客戶，故並無重大信貸風
   concentrations of credit risk, with exposure spread over a large              險集中的情況。
   number of counterparties and customers.

   EQUITY PRICE RISK                                                           股本價格風險
   Equity price risk is the risk that the fair values of equity securities     股本價格風險指股本指數水平及個別證券價
   decrease as a result of changes in the levels of equity indices and         值變動而導致的股本證券公允價值下跌的風
   the value of individual securities. The Group is exposed to equity          險。於 2022 年 12 月 31 日，本 集 團 面 臨 由 按

44. FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT OBJECTIVES                                     44. 金融風險管理目標及政策（續）
    AND POLICIES (Continued)
   CAPITAL MANAGEMENT                                                          資本管理
   The primary objectives of the Group’s capital management are to             本集團資本管理的主要目標為保障本集團繼
   safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as going concern and to           續以持續基準經營的能力，以及維持合理的
   maintain reasonable capital ratios in order to support its business         資本比率支持其業務，以及將股東的價值提
   and maximise shareholders’ value.                                           升至最高。

   The Group manages its capital structure and makes adjustments               本集團根據經濟狀況變動管理其資本架構及
   to it in light of changes in economic conditions. In order to optimise      作出調整。為優化債務及權益結餘，本集團
   the debt and equity balance, the Group may adjust the dividend              可調整支付予股東的股息、向股東退回資
   payment to shareholders, return capital to shareholders, issue new          本、發 行 新 股 或 新 債 或 贖 回 現 有 債 務。年
   shares or new debts or the redemption of existing debts. No                 內，管理資本的目標、政策或過程並無變動。
   changes were made in the objectives, policies or processes for
   managing capital during the year.

   The Group monitors capital using a net debt to equity ratio, which          本集團以債務淨額對權益比率監控資本，該
   is net debt divided by capital. Net debt includes borrowings,               比率為債務淨額除以資本。債務淨額包括借
   convertible loans and lease liabilities, less cash and bank deposits.       款、可換股貸款及租賃負債減現金及銀行存
   Capital represents the total equity.                                        款。資本乃指權益總額。

   The Group’s strategy is to maintain the net debt to equity ratio at a       本集團的策略為維持債務淨額對權益比率在
   healthy level in order to support its businesses. The principal             健康水平，以支持其業務。本集團採取的主
   strategies adopted by the Group include, without limitation,                要策略包括但不限於審閱未來現金流量需求
   reviewing future cash flow requirements and the ability to meet             及於債務到期時履行債務償還計劃的能力，
   debt repayment schedules when they fall due, maintaining a                  保持可用銀行融資在合理水平及調整投資計
   reasonable level of available banking facilities and adjusting              劃及融資計劃（如需要），以確保本集團擁有
   investment plans and financing plans, if necessary, to ensure that          合理水平的資本支持其業務。於報告期末的
   the Group has a reasonable level of capital to support its business.        債務淨額對權益比率如下：
   The net debt to equity ratio, at the end of the reporting period, is as
   follows:
